## 2.4 BALSAMS ⭐ (a "must-master" section — the syllabus names them explicitly)

### 2.4.1 Definition

📌 **DEFINITION** — **Balsams** are **resinous mixtures** that contain **large proportions of
benzoic acid, cinnamic acid, or their esters** (i.e. cinnamates/ benzoates).
They are **not** simply resin + volatile oil; the **presence of benzoic and/or cinnamic acid is
essential**.

⭐ **THE FOUR TRUE BALSAMS**

| Balsam | Contains |
|---|---|
| **Tolu balsam** | Benzoic + cinnamic acid (free & combined) |
| **Peru balsam** | Cinnamein (benzyl benzoate + benzyl cinnamate) |
| **Storax (Styrax)** | Cinnamic acid & its esters |
| **Benzoin** | Benzoic acid (Siam) / benzoic + cinnamic acid (Sumatra) |

⚠️ **NOT balsams (they lack benzoic/cinnamic acid) → they are OLEORESINS:**
**Copaiba balsam, Canada balsam, Gurjun balsam, Japan balsam, Mecca balsam.**

### 2.4.2 📋 MASTER MONOGRAPH TABLE — BALSAMS

| Drug | Synonyms | Biological source | Family | Chief constituents | Uses | Dose / Official preparations |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **BENZOIN** | **Gum benjamin, Loban, Benzoinum**; *Sumatra benzoin* & *Siam benzoin* | Balsamic resin obtained by incision of the stem of ***Styrax benzoin*** (Sumatra) and ***Styrax tonkinensis*** (Siam) | **Styracaceae** | **Sumatra:** free **benzoic + cinnamic acid**, coniferyl cinnamate, **sumaresinolic acid**, siaresinolic acid, phenylpropyl cinnamate, **vanillin**, styrol, styracin. **Siam:** ~ **benzoic acid only** (no cinnamic acid), **coniferyl benzoate (65–75 %), siaresinolic acid (19-hydroxy-oleanolic acid), vanillin** | ⭐ **Antiseptic, expectorant, protective (as tincture "compound tincture of benzoin"), carminative; inhalation for laryngitis & bronchitis; preservative for fats (antioxidant); incense; ingredient of Podophyllum paint** | **Compound Tincture of Benzoin (Friar's Balsam)** — inhalation **1 mL to 500 mL (1 pint) of hot water**; Benzoin 0.5–2 g; **Benzoic acid ointment (Whitfield's)** |
| **TOLU BALSAM** | Balsamum tolutanum, Thomas balsam | Balsam obtained from incised trunk of ***Myroxylon balsamum*** (var. *balsamum*) — Tolu, Colombia | **Leguminosae** | **Resin 75–80 % (toluresinotannol esters of benzoic & cinnamic acid)**, **free cinnamic acid (~ 12–15 %)** & **benzoic acid (~ 8 %)**, benzyl benzoate, **vanillin**, volatile oil traces | ⭐ **Expectorant & flavouring (Syrup of Tolu), mild antiseptic, ingredient of compound benzoin tincture; used in cough syrups & linctuses** | **Syrup of Tolu**; Tincture of Tolu **2–4 mL**; Balsam 0.5–2 g |
| **PERU BALSAM** | **Balsam of Peru**, Indian balsam, Black balsam, Balsamum peruvianum | Balsam obtained from the **scorched & beaten bark** of ***Myroxylon balsamum*** var. ***pereirae*** (*M. pereirae*) — chiefly **El Salvador** (⚠️ *not Peru*; only shipped from Peru) | **Leguminosae** | ⭐ **"Cinnamein" 50–65 %** = **benzyl benzoate + benzyl cinnamate**; **peruresinotannol** cinnamate & benzoate; free cinnamic acid; vanillin; nerolidol (peruviol). **Contains NO free benzoic acid of consequence & is NOT soluble in petroleum spirit** | ⭐ **Parasiticide (scabies), antiseptic, vulnerary — promotes granulation & wound healing (bed sores, ulcers, chapped nipples), local irritant, ingredient of ointments & suppositories for haemorrhoids; flavour & perfume fixative** | **Ointment of Peru balsam**; used **10–20 % topically**; internal dose (obsolete) 0.5–2 mL |
| **STORAX** | **Styrax, Prepared storax, Liquid storax, Sweet oriental gum, Levant/ American storax** | Balsam obtained from the **wood & inner bark** of ***Liquidambar orientalis*** (Levant/Asiatic) or ***L. styraciflua*** (American) | **Hamamelidaceae (Styracaceae in older texts — learn Hamamelidaceae)** | **Free cinnamic acid (~ 6–12 %), cinnamyl cinnamate (styracin), phenylpropyl cinnamate, storesin (storesinol) esters, ethyl & benzyl cinnamate, vanillin, styrene (styrol)** | ⭐ **Parasiticide (scabies), antiseptic, expectorant; ingredient of Compound Benzoin Tincture; perfumery fixative** | Ingredient of Comp. Benzoin Tincture; topical 10–20 % |

### 2.4.3 ⭐ COMPOUND TINCTURE OF BENZOIN — the exam's favourite formula

📌 **Compound Tincture of Benzoin B.P.** ("**Friar's Balsam**", "Jerusalem balsam",
"**Turlington's Balsam of Life**"):

| Ingredient | Quantity |
|---|---|
| **Benzoin (in coarse powder)** | **10 g** |
| **Prepared Storax** | **7.5 g** |
| **Tolu balsam** | **2.5 g** |
| **Aloes (in powder)** | **2.5 g** |
| **Alcohol (90 %)** | to **100 mL** |

🧠 **MEMORY HOOK — "10 – 7.5 – 2.5 – 2.5"** → **B**enzoin, **S**torax, **T**olu, **A**loes
("**B**ig **S**tar **T**akes **A**ll").
⭐ **Use:** as an **inhalation — 1 teaspoonful (about 1 mL, or 5 mL in some texts) added to
500 mL (1 pint) of hot (not boiling) water**, vapour inhaled; also a **protective & antiseptic
application to cracked skin**.
⚠️ Contains **Aloes** — a point often asked ("Which balsamic preparation contains aloes?").

### 2.4.4 🧪 IDENTIFICATION TESTS FOR BALSAMS ⭐⭐

| Test | Procedure | Result / Significance |
|---|---|---|
| **Test for benzoic/ cinnamic acid (general balsam test)** | Sublimation: heat a little balsam on a slide | **Needle-like crystals** of benzoic/ cinnamic acid sublime |
| **Potassium permanganate test** | Balsam + KMnO₄ → warm | Odour of **benzaldehyde (bitter almonds)** → **cinnamic acid present** (Peru, Storax, Sumatra benzoin) |
| **FeCl₃ test** | Alcoholic extract + FeCl₃ | Green colour → **resinotannol** present |
| **Peru balsam — solubility test** | Shake with **petroleum spirit/light petroleum** | ⭐ **Peru balsam is INSOLUBLE** (Copaiba & other oleoresins dissolve) |
| **Peru balsam — Alcoholic KOH/ methanolic KOH** | Balsam + alcoholic KOH & warm | Clear solution; addition of water → precipitate |
| **Peru balsam — Cinnamein layer** | Shake with ether, evaporate | Oily "cinnamein" residue, pleasant vanilla-like odour |
| **Tolu balsam** | + Sulphuric acid & alcohol | **Red colour**; solution in ether + KOH → crystals of potassium cinnamate |
| **Benzoin (Siam vs Sumatra)** | Add KMnO₄ | **Sumatra gives benzaldehyde odour** (cinnamic acid present); **Siam does NOT** (only benzoic acid) |
| **Benzoin + Ether + ammonia (or NaOH)** | — | Sumatra benzoin gives red-violet with H₂SO₄–formaldehyde |
| **Storax** | Cinnamic acid detected by KMnO₄ → benzaldehyde | Positive |
| **Gum absent test** | Add water | True balsams give **no mucilage** (unlike oleo-gum-resins) |

🧠 **MEMORY HOOK — "SUMATRA = SUM of both acids (benzoic + cinnamic); SIAM = Single acid
(benzoic only)."**

🔁 **COMPARE — SIAM vs SUMATRA BENZOIN** (frequent question)

| Feature | **SIAM Benzoin** | **SUMATRA Benzoin** |
|---|---|---|
| Botanical source | ***Styrax tonkinensis*** | ***Styrax benzoin*** |
| Acid present | **Benzoic acid only** | **Benzoic + Cinnamic acid** |
| Chief ester | **Coniferyl benzoate (65–75 %)** | Coniferyl cinnamate |
| Triterpene acid | **Siaresinolic acid** | **Sumaresinolic acid** |
| KMnO₄ test (benzaldehyde odour) | **Negative** | **Positive** |
| Form | Compressed pebble-like tears, brittle, yellow-brown | Reddish-brown to greyish-brown block with **whitish tears** |
| Odour | Vanilla-like (contains **vanillin**) | Balsamic, storax-like |
| Official/ preferred | Preferred for **internal & pharmaceutical** use | Used mainly in **inhalations/ external & perfumery** |

🔁 **COMPARE — TOLU vs PERU BALSAM**

| Feature | **TOLU BALSAM** | **PERU BALSAM** |
|---|---|---|
| Source | *Myroxylon balsamum* var. *balsamum* | *Myroxylon balsamum* var. *pereirae* |
| Geographical source | Colombia/Venezuela (Tolu) | **El Salvador** (shipped via Peru) |
| Method of collection | V-shaped incisions in trunk | **Bark scorched & beaten**, rags soaked, then boiled |
| Consistency | **Brown, brittle solid → soft plastic solid** | **Dark brown, viscous liquid** |
| Chief constituent | **Free cinnamic & benzoic acids + resin esters** | **Cinnamein (benzyl benzoate + benzyl cinnamate)** |
| Odour | Aromatic, vanilla-like | Balsamic, vanilla-like |
| Petroleum spirit | Partly soluble | **Insoluble** ⭐ |
| Chief use | **Expectorant & flavour (Syrup of Tolu)** | **Parasiticide & wound healing (scabies)** |

---

## 2.5 LATEX, DRIED JUICES & ALLIED EXUDATES

📌 **LATEX** — a milky, white or coloured fluid **emulsion** of terpenes, proteins, sugars, gums
and alkaloids present in **latex vessels/ laticiferous ducts**; it coagulates on exposure to air.

| Drug | Source | Family | Constituents | Uses / Dose |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **OPIUM** | Air-dried **milky exudate** from incised unripe **capsules** of ***Papaver somniferum*** | **Papaveraceae** | ⭐ **Morphine (≥ 9.5 %, IP NLT 9.5 %), Codeine (0.3–0.8 %), Thebaine, Papaverine (0.5–1 %), Narcotine/Noscapine (2–8 %), Narceine; meconic acid** (⭐ used to detect opium — **FeCl₃ gives reddish-purple**) | **Narcotic analgesic, sedative, antitussive, antidiarrhoeal.** Dose 30–120 mg. Preparations: **Tincture of Opium (Laudanum) 0.3–2 mL; Camphorated Tincture of Opium (Paregoric) 2–8 mL; Compound Powder of Opium; Dover's powder (Opium + Ipecac)**. ⚠️ **Narcotic — Schedule X/NDPS Act** |
| **PAPAIN** | Dried latex of unripe fruits of ***Carica papaya*** | **Caricaceae** | **Papain, chymopapain (proteolytic enzymes)** | **Protein digestant, meat tenderiser, de-worming, wound debridement, in dyspepsia; chymopapain for slipped disc** | 
| **ALOE** | Dried **juice** from the **leaves** of ***Aloe barbadensis*** (Curaçao), *A. ferox* (Cape), *A. perryi* (Socotrine) | **Liliaceae** | **Anthraquinone glycosides — Barbaloin (aloin), isobarbaloin, aloe-emodin, aloesin, resin** | **Purgative (stimulant), emmenagogue; gel = emollient & wound healing** Dose **100–300 mg** (Aloin 15–60 mg) |
| **KINO** | Dried juice from incisions in trunk of ***Pterocarpus marsupium*** | **Leguminosae** | **Kinotannic acid (~ 70–80 %), kino red, catechol, pyrocatechin** | **Astringent (diarrhoea), gargle** Dose 0.3–2 g |
| **CATECHU (Black/ Pale)** | **Black catechu (Kattha)** = aqueous extract of heartwood of ***Acacia catechu***; **Pale catechu (Gambier)** = extract of leaves & twigs of ***Uncaria gambier*** | **Leguminosae / Rubiaceae** | **Catechin, catechutannic acid (~ 25–35 %), quercetin, gambir fluorescein** | **Astringent — diarrhoea, gargle, mouthwash, dentifrice, tanning; in "paan"** Dose 0.3–2 g |
| **CARAGEENAN / IRISH MOSS** | *Chondrus crispus* | Gigartinaceae | Carrageenan (κ, λ, ι) | Suspending & emulsifying agent, demulcent, gel |
| **RUBBER (Latex)** | *Hevea brasiliensis* | Euphorbiaceae | cis-1,4-polyisoprene | Surgical gloves, tubing, closures, plasters, adhesive |
| **GUTTA PERCHA** | *Palaquium gutta* | Sapotaceae | trans-1,4-polyisoprene | **Dental root-canal filling**, splints |
| **ASCLEPIAS/ CALOTROPIS latex** | *Calotropis procera* | Asclepiadaceae | Calotropin, uscharin | Vesicant, purgative (folk) |

⭐ **Opium — indispensable numbers:** IP requires **NLT 9.5 % anhydrous morphine**;
**Powdered opium adjusted to 10 % morphine**; Morphine is the **chief & most abundant** alkaloid;
**Papaverine** = smooth-muscle relaxant (benzylisoquinoline); **Noscapine** = antitussive;
**Thebaine** = convulsant, precursor of **naloxone/ oxycodone**; **Meconic acid test** identifies
opium.

## 2.6 CONSOLIDATED 🔁 COMPARISON CHARTS

### A. Solubility fingerprint (learn this grid!)

| Substance | Water | Alcohol | Ether/Chloroform | Alkali |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Gum** | Soluble / swells | **Insoluble** | Insoluble | Soluble |
| **Mucilage** | Swells → slimy | Insoluble | Insoluble | — |
| **Resin** | **Insoluble** | **Soluble** | Soluble | Resin acids soluble |
| **Oleoresin** | Insoluble | Soluble | Soluble | Partly |
| **Oleo-gum-resin** | **Partly (emulsion/ milky)** | **Partly** | Partly | Partly |
| **Balsam** | Insoluble | **Soluble** | Soluble | Soluble (acids) |
| **Fixed oil** | Insoluble | Slightly (except castor — freely) | Soluble | Saponified |
| **Volatile oil** | Slightly (aromatic water) | **Freely soluble** | Soluble | — |

⭐ **NOTE:** ⚠️ **Castor oil is the only fixed oil freely soluble in alcohol** — classic MCQ.

### B. Which contains what?

| Constituent | Drugs containing it |
|---|---|
| **Benzoic acid** | Benzoin (Siam & Sumatra), Tolu, Peru (as benzyl benzoate) |
| **Cinnamic acid** | **Storax, Peru, Tolu, Sumatra benzoin** (NOT Siam benzoin) |
| **Vanillin** | Benzoin, Tolu, Peru, Storax (all four balsams!) |
| **Abietic acid** | **Colophony** |
| **Ferulic acid & umbelliferone** | **Asafoetida** |
| **Commiphoric acid** | **Myrrh** |
| **Guggulsterone** | **Guggul** |
| **Boswellic acid** | **Olibanum (Salai guggul)** |
| **Capsaicin** | **Capsicum** |
| **Gingerol/ shogaol** | **Ginger** |
| **Filicin/ filicic acid** | **Male fern** |
| **Podophyllotoxin & peltatins** | **Podophyllum** |
| **Jalapin & convolvulin** | **Jalap** |
| **Cinnamein** | **Peru balsam** |
| **Coniferyl benzoate** | **Siam benzoin** |
| **Coniferyl cinnamate** | **Sumatra benzoin** |
| **Styracin (cinnamyl cinnamate) & styrene** | **Storax** |
| **Meconic acid** | **Opium** |
| **Kinotannic acid** | **Kino** |
| **Catechutannic acid & catechin** | **Catechu** |
| **Barbaloin (aloin)** | **Aloe** |
| **Arabin (arabic acid salts) + oxidase** | **Acacia** |
| **Bassorin + tragacanthin** | **Tragacanth** |
| **Galactomannan** | **Guar gum** |
| **Agarose + agaropectin** | **Agar** |
| **Alginic acid (mannuronic + guluronic)** | **Alginates** |
| **Aleuritic acid** | **Shellac** |

### C. "Which is NOT a…" traps

| Statement | Truth |
|---|---|
| Copaiba/ Canada/ Gurjun balsam are balsams | ❌ **Oleoresins** |
| Tragacanth dissolves in water | ❌ **Swells only** |
| Acacia is a mucilage | ❌ It is a **gum** (pathological) |
| Isapghula is a gum | ❌ It is a **seed containing mucilage** |
| Agar is animal gelatin | ❌ **Seaweed (vegetable) — "vegetable gelatin"** |
| Colophony is an oleoresin | ❌ It is the **residual RESIN** after volatile oil is distilled off from oleoresin |
| Shellac is of plant origin | ❌ **Insect (animal) origin** |
| Peru balsam comes from Peru | ❌ Chiefly from **El Salvador** |
| Siam benzoin contains cinnamic acid | ❌ **Benzoic acid only** |
| Honey is a plant product | ❌ **Animal (bee) product** |
